SSC CGL MASTER LEARNING HIERARCHY (derived from Stage 2 PYQ evidence)
Read: [type | PYQ count | learning-order #]. Children listed in learning order.

[U]=unlocks [S]=question structures + traps.
Mastery: L1 recognize / L2 understand / L3 apply / L4 vary / L5 mix / L6 timed
(RECALL nodes: cue/prompt/cold/confusables/mixed/instant).
Frequency is shown per node but NEVER drives learning order.
